<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Inline numbered citation marker. Hover or focus shows a preview card with the</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Citation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // Portaled popups leave the DOM subtree that sets `dir`; carry the provider direction over.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Collapsible "Sources" summary with favicon stack and count, opening onto the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * numbered reference list. Inside a CitationGroup it shares the group's open</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
